# 离线五案：三版对齐器与两代范围规划器

两组人工对齐分别用各自目录 fixtures；模型组为五案 × 三版保存结果，分别输入两代适配器和规划器，均在本机离线运行。人工预期与独立语义审核不参与规划器输入。人工审核标准及标签定义见 [SEMANTIC_CRITERIA.md](SEMANTIC_CRITERIA.md)，来源见 [SOURCES.md](../v1_offline/SOURCES.md)。输入字段和人工标注变化会影响结果，不能将差异全归因于程序。

下列计数均非准确率：原始记录以 parsed.records 为分母；原始引用以每条 old_quotes/new_quotes 中每个元素为分母。源唯一有效要求来源可消歧、该 side 正确、原始磁盘路径可核对且引文在指定来源内唯一；它不证明引文支持语义。列表保留是适配器 resolved_*_quotes 含原引文文本；列表来源解析要求引文和 source_id 均保留；标量文本列只计 old_quote/new_quote 文本投影；标量来源有效列还核对 source_id、侧别、原始磁盘及唯一命中。v1 多引文可能保留在元数据列表但被标量规划器丢弃，v2 列表可送下游，来源绑定错误仍须暂缓。原模型未产生的引文不计入分母，另列缺引文的记录。

规划器动作和位置输出审核分母 134，匹配人工审核 134，未审核或结果变化 0；原模型记录以每版 parsed 记录总数为分母，逐记录标注列在各案。

## 独立人工审核统计

以下按每一条输出计数，评语来自独立人工审核，审查分母及逐条结论固定在 SEMANTIC_REVIEW.json；同一语义事实跨版本或保护位置会重复计数。判断用于研究，不是准确率；缺失的事实不在输出分母中。

| 原模型版本 | 原记录总数 | supported | partial_or_unknown | unsupported |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| v1 | 13 | 4 | 5 | 4 |
| v2 | 12 | 2 | 10 | 0 |
| v3 | 12 | 2 | 6 | 4 |

规划器动作（modify、preserve、uncertain、add）的分母与位置（editable_spans、protected_facts）分别列出，避免混算；appropriate_with_limit 的具体限制见逐条评语。

|---|---|---|---:|---|

| planner_v1 | human | modify | 2 | partially_supported=2 |
| planner_v1 | human | preserve | 6 | supported=6 |
| planner_v1 | human | protected_facts | 2 | supported=2 |
| planner_v1 | human | uncertain | 12 | appropriate=9, partially_supported=3 |
| planner_v1 | model | uncertain | 38 | appropriate_with_limit=38 |
| planner_v2 | human | add | 6 | supported=6 |
| planner_v2 | human | preserve | 5 | supported=5 |
| planner_v2 | human | protected_facts | 1 | supported=1 |
| planner_v2 | human | uncertain | 12 | appropriate=12 |
| planner_v2 | model | uncertain | 42 | appropriate_with_limit=42 |

## susan

旧记忆：Has a friend named ThomasSusan (referred to as 'Susan') who provides support and encouragement
来源：`a1332815-d6ba-4bdc-9ffa-657f7315d0ed`，`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_007/before.json`。

新证据：
- new-0：I've been thinking a lot about my career lately, and I've realized that my current role isn't supporting my well-being as much as I'd like. It's crucial for me to find a new job that offers a better work-life balance and aligns with my humanitarian goals.（`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_007/input.json#/raw_dialogue/0/content`）
- new-2：Susan's support has indeed been invaluable. Her encouragement helps me stay focused on my goals. And Daniel's insights have really pushed me to think outside the box in my career. It's been exciting to see how these connections have opened up new possibilities.（`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_007/input.json#/raw_dialogue/2/content`）

## skydiving

旧记忆：Dislikes extreme sports (e.g., skydiving) due to high injury risk, conflicting with commitment to safety and well-being
来源：`e1d4f8f9-0520-427d-94fd-a2ac0ee69d03`，`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_009/before.json`。

新证据：
- new-0：Hey, I recently had an interesting experience at a team-building event where I tried simulated skydiving. It was quite thrilling!（`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_009/input.json#/raw_dialogue/0/content`）
- new-2：Yeah, the adrenaline rush was something I didn't expect to enjoy so much. It's definitely made me more open to trying new things.（`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_009/input.json#/raw_dialogue/2/content`）
- new-8：For now, I'm just enjoying the thrill and seeing where it takes me. I still prioritize safety, of course.（`results/medium_dev/williams_native_prefix5_20261028_v3/users/2f1f897e-d67f-dbc5-6a7b-b7634a9e294f/session_009/input.json#/raw_dialogue/8/content`）

## 范围与限制

人工判断是研究者根据引文作出的初评，不能自动证明真实世界事实；`not_reviewed` 明确表示尚未完成逐项人工核对。模型输出中缺少的事实及由不同拆分粒度导致的遗漏，不能由逐条正确数反映。已有材料选取部分新对话，不代表全会话或全量数据。具体源文件、快照、上下文及 SimpleMem 覆盖差异见 SOURCES.md。

方法借鉴先判断后修订、时间有效性与条件重叠的思路；未调用或移植 ERASE、FactTrack、Cedar 的模块。日期区间和自然语言蕴含依赖上游明确标注；未知时间顺序不能从记录时间补成先后。
